# FR / NFR ↔ Code ↔ Test Matrix — 2026-09-18

**Generated:** 2026-09-18  
**Total unique FR/NFR IDs:** 1508  
**Source-of-truth inventory:** `docs/audits/_id_inventory_v3.json`  
**Generator:** `docs/audits/_gather_ids.py`  
**Machine version:** `docs/audits/fr-matrix.json`

This matrix is a full bidirectional audit: every `FR-*` and `NFR-*` identifier found anywhere in
the repository (specs, `FUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md`, traceability matrices, source code, tests)
is mapped to its spec source, implementing code, and covering test. NONE rows are the deliverable —
they flag what's missing, not what's implemented.

## Status legend

| Status | Meaning |
|--------|---------|
| `COVERED`          | spec + code + test all present |
| `IMPL-NO-TEST`     | spec + code present, no test reference |
| `SPEC-ONLY`        | spec only, no implementing code found in this scan |
| `CODE-ONLY-no-spec`| code only, no spec / traceability reference |

## Notes

- `spec` column = first spec-authority file that mentions the ID. Preferred order: 
  `agileplus-specs/*/spec.md` → `agileplus-specs/*/plan.md` → `FUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md` → 
  `PRD.md` → `docs/FR.md` / `docs/FR_DETAILED.md` → 
  `docs/reference/non-functional-requirements.md` → 
  `docs/traceability/TRACEABILITY_MATRIX.md` / `fr-3d-matrix.md` / `full-traceability-matrix.md` → 
  any other `docs/traceability/*.md`.
- `code refs` / `test refs` = up to 3 `file:line` entries shown; full list in JSON.
- Self-referential files (`docs/audits/*`, all `docs/**/fragemented/*`, 
  `docs/upstream-governance/*`, PR body / diff artifacts) are EXCLUDED from scanning so we don't
  cite ourselves or pull in third-party project docs.
- Range markers like `FR-CIV-LIFE-010..016` are normalized to their start (`FR-CIV-LIFE-010`).
